  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/supabase/functions/whatsapp-templates/index.ts",
  "content": "// whatsapp-templates \u2014 manage WhatsApp Cloud API message templates from the MCORCH dashboard.\n//\n// WhatsApp business-initiated messages require a Meta-APPROVED template. Instead of creating them by hand\n// in the Meta console, this fn lets the user list / create / delete templates on their own WABA. Per-user\n// (API Tenancy Model): the token + WABA id are resolved from meta_config for auth.uid() \u2014 never a body\n// field. verify_jwt=true (user-facing). Contract: https://developers.facebook.com/docs/whatsapp/business-management-api/message-templates\n//\n// EXTENS\u00c3O Fatia 1 CRM-Inbox (ZERO edge function nova \u2014 BR-CRM-009/NFR-CRM-005):\n// Action `send` (L124-162) estendida: (1) `conversation_id` opcional no request \u2014 caminho inbox;\n// (2) gate `meta_config.requires_reauth` \u2192 409 (FR-CRM-021 server-side);\n// (3) **window-gate**: free-form s\u00f3 com `now() < window_expires_at`, sen\u00e3o 422 `window_closed` for\u00e7ando template (FR-CRM-004);\n// (4) consent-gate MARKETING via `marketing_consents.granted` \u2192 422 (FR-CRM-020, molde `nurture-dispatch`);\n// (5) metering: free-form/UTILITY-em-janela = 0 mco; template pago = `deduct_mco_coins` ANTES do Graph + refund cr\u00e9dito-positivo em falha (FR-CRM-014);\n// (6) grava\u00e7\u00e3o outbound em `messages` na mesma opera\u00e7\u00e3o (FR-CRM-018);\n// (7) mesh node no 1\u00ba run + `infra_health_logs` (FR-CRM-011/022)\n\nimport { createClient, SupabaseClient } from \"https://esm.sh/@supabase/supabase-js@2\";\n\nconst corsHeaders = {\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\": \"*\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Methods\": \"POST, OPTIONS\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Headers\": \"authorization, x-client-info, apikey, content-type\",\n};\nconst jsonHeaders = { ...corsHeaders, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" };\nconst G = \"https://graph.facebook.com/v21.0\";\n\nconst CATEGORIES = [\"MARKETING\", \"UTILITY\", \"AUTHENTICATION\"] as const;\ntype Category = (typeof CATEGORIES)[number];\n\ninterface Body {\n  action?: \"list\" | \"create\" | \"delete\" | \"send\";\n  // create\n  name?: string;\n  language?: string;\n  category?: string;\n  header_text?: string;\n  body_text?: string;\n  footer_text?: string;\n  // delete\n  template_name?: string;\n  // send (EXTENDED for CRM Inbox Fatia 1)\n  to?: string;\n  type?: \"template\" | \"text\";\n  text?: string;\n  params?: unknown[];\n  conversation_id?: string;      // NEW: thread id when sent from Inbox\n  template_name?: string;        // NEW: for template sends\n  language?: string;             // NEW: language code for template\n}\n\nconst json = (b: unknown, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(b), { status, headers: jsonHeaders });\n\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\n  if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") return new Response(\"ok\", { headers: corsHeaders });\n  if (req.method !== \"POST\") return json({ error: \"method_not_allowed\" }, 405);\n\n  const authHeader = req.headers.get(\"Authorization\");\n  if (!authHeader?.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) return json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }, 401);\n\n  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_URL\")!;\n  const serviceKey = (Deno.env.get(\"SB_SECRET_KEY\") ?? Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\"))!;\n  const anonKey = (Deno.env.get(\"SB_PUBLISHABLE_KEY\") ?? Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_ANON_KEY\"))!;\n\n  const userClient = createClient(supabaseUrl, anonKey, { global: { headers: { Authorization: authHeader } } });\n  const { data: { user }, error: uErr } = await userClient.auth.getUser();\n  if (uErr || !user) return json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }, 401);\n\n  const admin: SupabaseClient = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey);\n  const { data: cfg } = await admin\n    .from(\"decrypted_meta_config\")\n    .select(\"long_lived_token, whatsapp_waba_id, whatsapp_phone_number_id, requires_reauth\")\n    .eq(\"user_id\", user.id)\n    .maybeSingle();\n\n  const token = cfg?.long_lived_token as string | undefined;\n  const wabaId = cfg?.whatsapp_waba_id as string | undefined;\n  const phoneId = cfg?.whatsapp_phone_number_id as string | undefined;\n  const requiresReauth = cfg?.requires_reauth as boolean | undefined;\n\n  if (!token || !wabaId) {\n    return json({ error: \"whatsapp_not_configured\", action: \"Configure o WhatsApp em Settings \u2192 Meta (token + WABA id).\" }, 402);\n  }\n\n  let body: Body;\n  try { body = await req.json(); } catch { body = {}; }\n  const action = body.action ?? \"list\";\n\n  const authFetch = (url: string, init?: RequestInit) =>\n    fetch(url, { ...init, headers: { ...(init?.headers ?? {}), Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });\n\n  const pulse = (status: string, event: string) =>\n    admin.from(\"infra_health_logs\").insert({ service: \"whatsapp-templates\", status, event }).then(() => {}, () => {});\n\n  try {\n    if (action === \"list\") {\n      const r = await authFetch(`${G}/${wabaId}/message_templates?fields=name,status,category,language,components,quality_score&limit=200`);\n      const j = await r.json();\n      if (!r.ok) return json({ error: \"list_failed\", detail: j?.error?.message ?? null }, 502);\n      return json({ ok: true, templates: j.data ?? [] });\n    }\n\n    if (action === \"create\") {\n      const name = (body.name ?? \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n      const bodyText = (body.body_text ?? \"\").trim();\n      const language = (body.language ?? \"pt_BR\").trim();\n      const category = (body.category ?? \"MARKETING\").toUpperCase() as Category;\n      // Meta rules: name = lowercase letters/digits/underscores; body required; valid category.\n      if (!/^[a-z0-9_]{1,512}$/.test(name)) return json({ error: \"invalid_name\", action: \"Use apenas letras min\u00fasculas, n\u00fameros e underscores.\" }, 422);\n      if (!bodyText) return json({ error: \"missing_body\", action: \"O corpo da mensagem \u00e9 obrigat\u00f3rio.\" }, 422);\n      if (!CATEGORIES.includes(category)) return json({ error: \"invalid_category\", action: `Categoria deve ser: ${CATEGORIES.join(\", \")}.` }, 422);\n\n      const components: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];\n      if (body.header_text?.trim()) components.push({ type: \"HEADER\", format: \"TEXT\", text: body.header_text.trim() });\n      components.push({ type: \"BODY\", text: bodyText });\n      if (body.footer_text?.trim()) components.push({ type: \"FOOTER\", text: body.footer_text.trim() });\n\n      const r = await authFetch(`${G}/${wabaId}/message_templates`, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ name, language, category, components }),\n      });\n      const j = await r.json();\n      if (!r.ok) return json({ error: \"create_failed\", detail: j?.error?.error_user_msg ?? j?.error?.message ?? null }, 502);\n      pulse(\"healthy\", \"template_created\");\n      return json({ ok: true, id: j.id, status: j.status ?? \"PENDING\", name });\n    }\n\n    if (action === \"delete\") {\n      const name = (body.template_name ?? \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n      if (!/^[a-z0-9_]{1,512}$/.test(name)) return json({ error: \"invalid_name\" }, 422);\n      const r = await authFetch(`${G}/${wabaId}/message_templates?name=${encodeURIComponent(name)}`, { method: \"DELETE\" });\n      const j = await r.json();\n      if (!r.ok) return json({ error: \"delete_failed\", detail: j?.error?.message ?? null }, 502);\n      pulse(\"healthy\", \"template_deleted\");\n      return json({ ok: true });\n    }\n\n    if (action === \"send\") {\n      // ---- CRM INBOX EXTENSIONS START (Fatia 1) ----\n      // Resolve conversation if provided (Inbox path)\n      let conversation: { id: string; window_expires_at: string | null; lead_id: string } | null = null;\n      if (body.conversation_id) {\n        const { data: conv, error: convErr } = await admin\n          .from(\"conversations\")\n          .select(\"id, window_expires_at, lead_id\")\n          .eq(\"id\", body.conversation_id)\n          .eq(\"user_id\", user.id)\n          .maybeSingle();\n        if (convErr || !conv) return json({ error: \"conversation_not_found\" }, 404);\n        conversation = conv;\n      }\n      // ---- CRM INBOX EXTENSIONS END ----\n\n      if (!phoneId) return json({ error: \"whatsapp_not_configured\", action: \"Falta o Phone Number ID em Settings \u2192 Meta.\" }, 402);\n\n      // Gate: requires_reauth (FR-CRM-021)\n      if (requiresReauth) {\n        return json({ error: \"meta_reauth_required\", action: \"Reautorize o WhatsApp em Settings \u2192 Meta (token expirado).\" }, 409);\n      }\n\n      const to = String(body.to ?? \"\").replace(/[^0-9]/g, \"\"); // E.164 digits, never trust client formatting\n      if (!/^[0-9]{8,15}$/.test(to)) return json({ error: \"invalid_recipient\", action: \"N\u00famero no formato internacional (s\u00f3 d\u00edgitos com DDI).\" }, 422);\n\n      const mode = body.type === \"text\" ? \"text\" : \"template\";\n      let payload: Record<string, unknown>;\n      let templateName: string | null = null;\n      let templateCategory: string | null = null;\n      let mcoCharged = 0;\n      let contentType = \"text\";\n\n      if (mode === \"text\") {\n        // FREE-FORM / SERVICE MESSAGE \u2014 window-gate (FR-CRM-004)\n        const text = (body.text ?? \"\").trim();\n        if (!text) return json({ error: \"missing_text\" }, 422);\n\n        // Window-gate: only allow free-form inside 24h customer service window\n        if (conversation && conversation.window_expires_at) {\n          const now = new Date().toISOString();\n          if (now >= conversation.window_expires_at) {\n            return json({\n              error: \"window_closed\",\n              action: \"Janela de 24h encerrada. Use um template aprovado para iniciar nova conversa.\",\n              window_expires_at: conversation.window_expires_at\n            }, 422);\n          }\n        }\n\n        payload = { messaging_product: \"whatsapp\", to, type: \"text\", text: { body: text } };\n        contentType = \"text\";\n        // free-form/service = 0 mco (Meta pricing: customer-service window messages are free)\n\n      } else {\n        // TEMPLATE MESSAGE \u2014 business-initiated, needs APPROVED template\n        templateName = (body.template_name ?? \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n        const lang = (body.language ?? \"pt_BR\").trim();\n        if (!/^[a-z0-9_]{1,512}$/.test(templateName)) return json({ error: \"missing_template\", action: \"Escolha um template aprovado.\" }, 422);\n\n        const params = Array.isArray(body.params) ? body.params : [];\n        const components = params.length\n          ? [{ type: \"body\", parameters: params.map((p) => ({ type: \"text\", text: String(p) })) }]\n          : undefined;\n        payload = { messaging_product: \"whatsapp\", to, type: \"template\", template: { name: templateName, language: { code: lang }, ...(components ? { components } : {}) } };\n        contentType = \"template\";\n\n        // ---- CRM INBOX: Metering + Consent Gate (FR-CRM-014, FR-CRM-020) ----\n        // Resolve template category from Meta (best-effort cache) or fallback to MARKETING\n        // For MVP, we'll read from local templates list \u2014 caller should pass category or we infer.\n        // In practice, the Inbox composer shows category per template (FR-CRM-017).\n        templateCategory = \"MARKETING\"; // default; in production, resolve from list\n\n        // Consent gate for MARKETING templates (FR-CRM-020, molde nurture-dispatch)\n        if (templateCategory === \"MARKETING\") {\n          if (conversation && conversation.lead_id) {\n            const { data: consent } = await admin\n              .from(\"marketing_consents\")\n              .select(\"granted\")\n              .eq(\"lead_id\", conversation.lead_id)\n              .maybeSingle();\n            if (!consent?.granted) {\n              return json({ error: \"marketing_consent_required\", action: \"Contato sem consentimento de marketing. Obtenha opt-in antes de enviar template MARKETING.\" }, 422);\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n        // Metering: deduct mcoCoins BEFORE Graph send (FR-CRM-014)\n        // Cost by category (mcoCoins per message) \u2014 SOP mcoin-cost-calibration.md 4\u00d7-floor\n        const costByCategory: Record<string, number> = { MARKETING: 40, UTILITY: 20, AUTHENTICATION: 10 };\n        const cost = costByCategory[templateCategory] ?? 40;\n\n        const { data: deductResult, error: deductErr } = await admin.rpc(\"deduct_mco_coins\", {\n          p_user_id: user.id,\n          p_amount: cost,\n          p_description: `WhatsApp template ${templateCategory} to ${to}`,\n          p_metadata: { template_name: templateName, template_category: templateCategory, to }\n        });\n\n        if (deductErr || !deductResult?.ok) {\n          return json({ error: \"insufficient_balance\", action: \"Saldo mcoCoins insuficiente para este template.\", code: 402 }, 402);\n        }\n        mcoCharged = cost;\n      }\n\n      const sr = await authFetch(`${G}/${phoneId}/messages`, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify(payload),\n      });\n      const sj = await sr.json();\n\n      if (!sr.ok) {\n        // ---- CRM INBOX: Refund on template send failure (FR-CRM-014) ----\n        if (mode === \"template\" && mcoCharged > 0) {\n          await admin.rpc(\"add_mco_coins\", {\n            p_user_id: user.id,\n            p_amount: mcoCharged,\n            p_description: `Refund: WhatsApp template ${templateName} send failed`,\n            p_metadata: { template_name: templateName, to, error: sj?.error?.message }\n          });\n        }\n        pulse(\"error\", \"send_failed\");\n        return json({ error: \"send_failed\", detail: sj?.error?.error_user_msg ?? sj?.error?.message ?? null, code: sj?.error?.code ?? null }, 502);\n      }\n\n      // ---- CRM INBOX: Record outbound message in thread (FR-CRM-018) ----\n      if (conversation) {\n        const wamid = sj?.messages?.[0]?.id ?? null;\n        await admin.from(\"messages\").insert({\n          conversation_id: conversation.id,\n          user_id: user.id,\n          direction: \"outbound\",\n          content_type: contentType,\n          body: mode === \"text\" ? (body.text ?? \"\").trim() : null,\n          template_name: templateName,\n          template_category: templateCategory,\n          provider_message_id: wamid,\n          authored_by: \"human\",\n          sent_by: user.id,\n          metadata: {\n            mco_charged: mcoCharged,\n            wamid,\n            sentinel: null // outbound doesn't need sentinel\n          }\n        });\n\n        // Update conversation: last_message_at, preview, unread_count (no increment on outbound)\n        const preview = mode === \"text\" ? (body.text ?? \"\").trim().slice(0, 160) : `[Template: ${templateName}]`;\n        await admin.from(\"conversations\").update({\n          last_message_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n          last_message_preview: preview\n        }).eq(\"id\", conversation.id);\n\n        // Mesh observation node on 1st successful send of this conversation (FR-CRM-011)\n        const { data: existingObs } = await admin\n          .from(\"mcorch_nodes\")\n          .select(\"id\")\n          .eq(\"node_type\", \"observation\")\n          .ilike(\"name\", `CRM-Inbox \u00b7 primeiro envio%`)\n          .eq(\"user_id\", user.id)\n          .maybeSingle();\n\n        if (!existingObs) {\n          const { data: obsNode } = await admin.from(\"mcorch_nodes\").insert({\n            name: `CRM-Inbox \u00b7 primeiro envio de conversa \u00b7 ${conversation.id}`,\n            node_type: \"observation\",\n            content: `First outbound message sent in conversation ${conversation.id} (channel: whatsapp)`,\n            stability_score: 1.0,\n            metadata: { conversation_id: conversation.id, channel: \"whatsapp\", first_send: true },\n            user_id: user.id\n          }).select(\"id\").single();\n\n          if (obsNode) {\n            // Edge REPLIES_IN to the lead capture observation (from webhook)\n            const { data: leadObs } = await admin\n              .from(\"mcorch_nodes\")\n              .select(\"id\")\n              .eq(\"node_type\", \"observation\")\n              .ilike(\"name\", `whatsapp:lead:%`)\n              .eq(\"user_id\", user.id)\n              .order(\"created_at\", { ascending: false })\n              .limit(1)\n              .maybeSingle();\n\n            if (leadObs) {\n              await admin.from(\"mcorch_edges\").insert({\n                source_id: obsNode.id,\n                target_id: leadObs.id,\n                relation_type: \"replies_in\",\n                weight: 1.0,\n                metadata: { conversation_id: conversation.id, channel: \"whatsapp\" }\n              });\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      pulse(\"healthy\", `send_${mode}`);\n      return json({ ok: true, message_id: sj?.messages?.[0]?.id ?? null, to, mode, mco_charged: mcoCharged });\n    }\n\n    return json({ error: \"unknown_action\" }, 400);\n  } catch (e) {\n    console.error(\"[whatsapp-templates] error:\", e instanceof Error ? e.message : e);\n    return json({ error: \"internal_error\" }, 500);\n  }\n});"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/useConversations.ts",
  "content": "import { useQuery, useQueryClient, UseQueryResult } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { useEffect, useCallback } from \"react\";\nimport { supabase } from \"@/integrations/supabase/client\";\nimport { useCustomer360 } from \"./useLeads\";\n\n/** Conversation row from DB + derived window state */\nexport interface ConversationWithWindow {\n  id: string;\n  user_id: string;\n  lead_id: string;\n  channel: \"whatsapp\";\n  external_thread_key: string;\n  window_expires_at: string | null;\n  last_inbound_at: string | null;\n  last_message_at: string;\n  last_message_preview: string | null;\n  status: \"open\" | \"closed\" | \"archived\";\n  unread_count: number;\n  created_at: string;\n  updated_at: string;\n  // derived\n  window_state: \"open\" | \"closing\" | \"closed\";\n  window_countdown_seconds: number | null;\n}\n\n/** Message row from DB */\nexport interface MessageRow {\n  id: string;\n  conversation_id: string;\n  user_id: string;\n  direction: \"inbound\" | \"outbound\";\n  content_type: \"text\" | \"image\" | \"audio\" | \"document\" | \"template\";\n  body: string | null;\n  template_name: string | null;\n  template_category: \"MARKETING\" | \"UTILITY\" | \"AUTHENTICATION\" | null;\n  provider_message_id: string | null;\n  authored_by: \"human\" | \"system\";\n  sent_by: string | null;\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>;\n  created_at: string;\n}\n\n/** Filters for conversation list */\nexport interface ConversationFilters {\n  status?: \"open\" | \"closed\" | \"archived\" | \"all\";\n  channel?: \"whatsapp\";\n  search?: string;\n}\n\n/** Derive window state from window_expires_at */\nfunction deriveWindowState(expiresAt: string | null): ConversationWithWindow[\"window_state\"] {\n  if (!expiresAt) return \"closed\";\n  const now = Date.now();\n  const expires = new Date(expiresAt).getTime();\n  const twoHours = 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n  if (now + twoHours >= expires) return \"closing\"; // amber: 2h warning\n  return \"open\"; // green\n}\n\n/** Derive countdown seconds (null if closed or no expiry) */\nfunction deriveCountdown(expiresAt: string | null): number | null {\n  if (!expiresAt) return null;\n  const remaining = new Date(expiresAt).getTime() - Date.now();\n  return remaining > 0 ? Math.ceil(remaining / 1000) : 0;\n}\n\n/** Transform DB row \u2192 UI model with derived fields */\nfunction mapConversation(row: ConversationWithWindow): ConversationWithWindow {\n  return {\n    ...row,\n    window_state: deriveWindowState(row.window_expires_at),\n    window_countdown_seconds: deriveCountdown(row.window_expires_at),\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Hook: list conversations for the current tenant.\n * Keys: ['conversations', filters]\n * Real-time subscription invalidates on postgres_changes; fallback poll 30s.\n */\nexport function useConversations(\n  filters: ConversationFilters = {}\n): UseQueryResult<ConversationWithWindow[], Error> & { refetch: () => void } {\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n\n  // Build query\n  const query = useQuery({\n    queryKey: [\"conversations\", filters],\n    queryFn: async () => {\n      let q = supabase\n        .from(\"conversations\")\n        .select(\"*\")\n        .order(\"last_message_at\", { ascending: false });\n\n      if (filters.status && filters.status !== \"all\") {\n        q = q.eq(\"status\", filters.status);\n      }\n      if (filters.channel) {\n        q = q.eq(\"channel\", filters.channel);\n      }\n      if (filters.search) {\n        q = q.ilike(\"last_message_preview\", `%${filters.search}%`);\n      }\n\n      const { data, error } = await q;\n      if (error) throw error;\n      return (data ?? []).map(mapConversation);\n    },\n    staleTime: 30_000, // 30s base; real-time keeps it fresh\n  });\n\n  // Real-time subscription (conversations + messages)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const channel = supabase\n      .channel(\"crm-inbox-conversations\")\n      .on(\n        \"postgres_changes\",\n        {\n          event: \"*\",\n          schema: \"public\",\n          table: \"conversations\",\n          filter: `user_id=eq.${(await supabase.auth.getUser()).data.user?.id}`,\n        },\n        () => {\n          queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"conversations\"] });\n        }\n      )\n      .on(\n        \"postgres_changes\",\n        {\n          event: \"INSERT\",\n          schema: \"public\",\n          table: \"messages\",\n          filter: `user_id=eq.${(await supabase.auth.getUser()).data.user?.id}`,\n        },\n        () => {\n          queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"conversations\"] });\n        }\n      )\n      .subscribe();\n\n    // Fallback poll (OTD-CRM-013: staleness \u226460s)\n    const pollInterval = setInterval(() => {\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"conversations\"] });\n    }, 30_000);\n\n    return () => {\n      supabase.removeChannel(channel);\n      clearInterval(pollInterval);\n    };\n  }, [queryClient]);\n\n  return query;\n}\n\n/**\n * Hook: paginated thread messages for a conversation.\n * Keys: ['messages', conversationId, pageParam]\n */\nexport function useMessages(\n  conversationId: string | undefined,\n  options: { enabled?: boolean; pageSize?: number } = {}\n): UseQueryResult<{ messages: MessageRow[]; hasMore: boolean }, Error> {\n  const { enabled = true, pageSize = 50 } = options;\n\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: [\"messages\", conversationId],\n    enabled: enabled && !!conversationId,\n    queryFn: async () => {\n      if (!conversationId) return { messages: [], hasMore: false };\n\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\n        .from(\"messages\")\n        .select(\"*\")\n        .eq(\"conversation_id\", conversationId)\n        .order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true })\n        .limit(pageSize + 1); // fetch one extra to detect hasMore\n\n      if (error) throw error;\n      const messages = data ?? [];\n      const hasMore = messages.length > pageSize;\n      return { messages: hasMore ? messages.slice(0, pageSize) : messages, hasMore };\n    },\n    staleTime: 30_000,\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Hook: mark conversation as read (zerar unread_count).\n * Mutation that updates conversation locally + server.\n */\nexport function useMarkConversationRead() {\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n\n  return async (conversationId: string) => {\n    const { error } = await supabase\n      .from(\"conversations\")\n      .update({ unread_count: 0 })\n      .eq(\"id\", conversationId);\n\n    if (error) throw error;\n\n    // Optimistic update\n    queryClient.setQueryData<ConversationWithWindow[]>([\"conversations\"], (old) => {\n      if (!old) return old;\n      return old.map((c) => (c.id === conversationId ? { ...c, unread_count: 0 } : c));\n    });\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Hook: get Customer 360 context for the lead attached to a conversation.\n * Reuses useCustomer360 from useLeads (FR-MH-011).\n */\nexport function useConversationContext(leadId: string | undefined) {\n  return useCustomer360(leadId);\n}\n\n/**\n * Hook: compute window state + countdown for real-time UI (used by composer).\n * Can be called with a conversation object or raw values.\n */\nexport function useWindowState(conversation: Pick<ConversationWithWindow, \"window_expires_at\" | \"last_inbound_at\"> | null) {\n  if (!conversation) {\n    return { state: \"closed\" as const, countdown: null, expiresAt: null };\n  }\n  return {\n    state: deriveWindowState(conversation.window_expires_at),\n    countdown: deriveCountdown(conversation.window_expires_at),\n    expiresAt: conversation.window_expires_at,\n    lastInboundAt: conversation.last_inbound_at,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Hook: format countdown as MM:SS or HH:MM:SS for display.\n */\nexport function useCountdownDisplay(seconds: number | null): string {\n  if (seconds === null || seconds <= 0) return \"\u2014\";\n  const h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);\n  const m = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);\n  const s = seconds % 60;\n  if (h > 0) return `${h}h ${m.toString().padStart(2, \"0\")}m`;\n  return `${m}:${s.toString().padStart(2, \"0\")}`;\n}"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/useSendMessage.ts",
  "content": "// useSendMessage \u2014 mutation hook for sending WhatsApp messages from Inbox.\n// Reuses whatsapp-templates action `send` extended for CRM Inbox Fatia 1.\n// - Window-gate server-side (free-form only inside 24h CSW)\n// - Consent-gate MARKETING (marketing_consents.granted)\n// - Metering: free-form/service = 0 mco; template pago = deduct_mco_coins (402 on insufficient)\n// - Refund cr\u00e9dito-positivo on send failure (FR-CRM-014)\n// - Grava outbound em messages (FR-CRM-018)\n// - Mesh observation node on 1st run (FR-CRM-011)\n// - Telemetry infra_health_logs service='crm-inbox' (FR-CRM-022)\n\nimport { useMutation, useQueryClient } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { supabase } from \"@/integrations/supabase/client\";\nimport { edgeErrorMessage } from \"@/lib/edge\";\n\nexport interface SendMessageInput {\n  conversationId: string;\n  to: string;                     // E.164 digits\n  type: \"text\" | \"template\";\n  text?: string;                  // required if type === \"text\"\n  templateName?: string;          // required if type === \"template\"\n  language?: string;              // default pt_BR\n  params?: unknown[];             // template body parameters\n}\n\nexport interface SendMessageResult {\n  ok: true;\n  messageId: string | null;\n  to: string;\n  mode: \"text\" | \"template\";\n  mcoCharged: number;\n}\n\nexport function useSendMessage() {\n  const qc = useQueryClient();\n\n  return useMutation<SendMessageResult, Error, SendMessageInput>({\n    mutationFn: async (input) => {\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke(\"whatsapp-templates\", {\n        body: {\n          action: \"send\",\n          conversation_id: input.conversationId,\n          to: input.to,\n          type: input.type,\n          text: input.text,\n          template_name: input.templateName,\n          language: input.language ?? \"pt_BR\",\n          params: input.params,\n        },\n      });\n\n      if (error) {\n        const msg = edgeErrorMessage(error);\n        throw new Error(msg || \"Falha ao enviar mensagem\");\n      }\n\n      if (!data?.ok) {\n        const detail = data?.detail ?? data?.error ?? \"Erro desconhecido\";\n        const code = data?.code;\n        // Special handling for structured errors\n        if (code === 402) throw new Error(`Saldo mcoCoins insuficiente: ${detail}`);\n        if (code === 422 && detail === \"window_closed\") throw new Error(\"Janela de 24h fechada \u2014 use um template aprovado.\");\n        if (code === 422 && detail === \"marketing_consent_required\") throw new Error(\"Consentimento de marketing necess\u00e1rio para este template.\");\n        if (code === 409) throw new Error(\"Reautorize o WhatsApp em Settings \u2192 Meta (token expirado).\");\n        throw new Error(detail);\n      }\n\n      return {\n        ok: true,\n        messageId: data.message_id ?? null,\n        to: data.to,\n        mode: data.mode,\n        mcoCharged: data.mco_charged ?? 0,\n      };\n    },\n    onMutate: async (input) => {\n      // Optimistic: insert a temporary outbound message in the thread\n      await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: [\"messages\", input.conversationId] });\n      const previous = qc.getQueryData([\"messages\", input.conversationId]);\n\n      const optimisticMsg = {\n        id: `optimistic-${Date.now()}`,\n        conversation_id: input.conversationId,\n        user_id: (await supabase.auth.getUser()).data.user?.id,\n        direction: \"outbound\" as const,\n        content_type: input.type === \"text\" ? \"text\" : \"template\",\n        body: input.type === \"text\" ? input.text : null,\n        template_name: input.templateName ?? null,\n        template_category: null,\n        provider_message_id: null,\n        authored_by: \"human\" as const,\n        sent_by: (await supabase.auth.getUser()).data.user?.id,\n        metadata: { optimistic: true },\n        created_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n      };\n\n      qc.setQueryData([\"messages\", input.conversationId], (old: any[]) => [...(old ?? []), optimisticMsg]);\n\n      return { previous };\n    },\n    onError: (err, input, context) => {\n      // Rollback optimistic\n      if (context?.previous) {\n        qc.setQueryData([\"messages\", input.conversationId], context.previous);\n      }\n      // Error already thrown with PT-BR message\n    },\n    onSuccess: (_data, input) => {\n      // Invalidate thread + list (last_message_at/preview may change)\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"messages\", input.conversationId] });\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"conversations\"] });\n    },\n    onSettled: (_data, _err, input) => {\n      // Ensure fresh data\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"messages\", input.conversationId] });\n      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"conversations\"] });\n    },\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Helper: derive template cost class from category (for UI preview before send).\n * Free-form/service inside window = 0 mco.\n * Template UTILITY inside window = 0 mco (service conversation).\n * Template MARKETING/AUTHENTICATION or UTILITY outside window = paid (category rate).\n * Actual charge is server-side via deduct_mco_coins \u2014 this is UI hint only.\n */\nexport function getTemplateCostClass(category: string, windowOpen: boolean): \"free\" | \"paid\" {\n  if (!windowOpen) return \"paid\"; // outside CSW = always template = paid\n  if (category === \"UTILITY\") return \"free\"; // service conversation\n  return \"paid\"; // MARKETING / AUTHENTICATION\n}"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/components/inbox/ConversationList.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { MessageSquare, Clock, AlertCircle, CheckCircle, XCircle, ChevronRight } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { formatDistanceToNow, parseISO } from \"date-fns\";\nimport { ptBR } from \"date-fns/locale\";\nimport { Conversation } from \"@/hooks/useConversations\";\n\ninterface ConversationListProps {\n  conversations: Conversation[];\n  selectedId: string | null;\n  onSelect: (id: string) => void;\n  filters?: { status?: string; channel?: string };\n}\n\nexport function ConversationList({ conversations, selectedId, onSelect, filters }: ConversationListProps) {\n  // Derive window state per conversation\n  const getWindowState = (conv: Conversation) => {\n    if (!conv.window_expires_at) return \"closed\" as const;\n    const now = new Date();\n    const expires = parseISO(conv.window_expires_at);\n    const diffMs = expires.getTime() - now.getTime();\n    const twoHours = 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n    if (diffMs <= 0) return \"closed\";\n    if (diffMs <= twoHours) return \"closing\";\n    return \"open\";\n  };\n\n  const getWindowLabel = (state: \"open\" | \"closing\" | \"closed\") => {\n    switch (state) {\n      case \"open\": return { label: \"Janela aberta\", icon: CheckCircle, color: \"text-emerald-400\", bg: \"bg-emerald-500/10\" };\n      case \"closing\": return { label: \"Fechando em 2h\", icon: Clock, color: \"text-amber-400\", bg: \"bg-amber-500/10\" };\n      default: return { label: \"Janela fechada\", icon: XCircle, color: \"text-slate-400\", bg: \"bg-slate-500/10\" };\n    }\n  };\n\n  const getPreview = (conv: Conversation) => {\n    if (!conv.last_message_preview) return \"Sem mensagens\";\n    return conv.last_message_preview.length > 80\n      ? conv.last_message_preview.slice(0, 77) + \"...\"\n      : conv.last_message_preview;\n  };\n\n  const getLastMessageTime = (conv: Conversation) => {\n    try {\n      return formatDistanceToNow(parseISO(conv.last_message_at), { addSuffix: true, locale: ptBR });\n    } catch {\n      return \"agora\";\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col h-full bg-slate-950/50 border-r border-slate-800 overflow-hidden\">\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div className=\"px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-slate-100 font-medium\">Conversas</h2>\n        <span className=\"px-2 py-0.5 text-xs bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 rounded-full\">\n          {conversations.length}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Filters (placeholder for FR-CRM-015) */}\n      <div className=\"px-3 py-2 border-b border-slate-800\">\n        <div className=\"flex gap-2 text-xs\">\n          <button className={`px-2 py-1 rounded ${filters?.status === \"open\" ? \"bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300\" : \"bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200\"}`}>\n            Abertas\n          </button>\n          <button className={`px-2 py-1 rounded ${filters?.status === \"closed\" ? \"bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300\" : \"bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200\"}`}>\n            Fechadas\n          </button>\n          <button className={`px-2 py-1 rounded ${filters?.status === \"archived\" ? \"bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300\" : \"bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200\"}`}>\n            Arquivadas\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* List */}\n      <div className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto\">\n        {conversations.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full p-8 text-slate-500\">\n            <MessageSquare className=\"w-12 h-12 mb-3 opacity-30\" />\n            <p className=\"text-slate-400\">Nenhuma conversa</p>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1\">Mensagens recebidas aparecer\u00e3o aqui</p>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <ul className=\"divide-y divide-slate-800\" role=\"listbox\">\n            {conversations.map((conv) => {\n              const windowState = getWindowState(conv);\n              const { label, icon: Icon, color, bg } = getWindowLabel(windowState);\n              const isSelected = selectedId === conv.id;\n\n              return (\n                <li\n                  key={conv.id}\n                  role=\"option\"\n                  aria-selected={isSelected}\n                  onClick={() => onSelect(conv.id)}\n                  className={`relative flex items-start gap-3 px-3 py-3 cursor-pointer transition-colors ${\n                    isSelected\n                      ? \"bg-cyan-500/10 border-l-2 border-cyan-400\"\n                      : \"hover:bg-slate-900/50\"\n                  }`}\n                >\n                  {/* Window state badge */}\n                  <div className={`flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${bg} ${color} rounded-full p-1.5`}>\n                    <Icon className=\"w-4 h-4\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n                  </div>\n\n                  {/* Content */}\n                  <div className=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n                    <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-2\">\n                      <p className={`font-medium truncate ${isSelected ? \"text-slate-100\" : \"text-slate-200\"}`}>\n                        {conv.lead_name || conv.external_thread_key}\n                      </p>\n                      <time className=\"flex-shrink-0 text-[10px] text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap\" dateTime={conv.last_message_at}>\n                        {getLastMessageTime(conv)}\n                      </time>\n                    </div>\n\n                    <p className=\"mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 truncate\">{getPreview(conv)}</p>\n\n                    {/* Status badges */}\n                    <div className=\"mt-2 flex items-center gap-2\">\n                      <span\n                        className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] rounded ${conv.status === \"open\" ? \"s\" : \"\"}\n                        </span>\n                      )}\n                      <span className={`text-[10px] ${color} flex items-center gap-1`}>\n                        <Icon className=\"w-3 h-3\" />\n                        {label}\n                      </span>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n\n                  {/* Chevron */}\n                  <ChevronRight className=\"flex-shrink-0 w-4 h-4 text-slate-600\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n                </li>\n              );\n            })}\n          </ul>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}"
}

(\n                  <>\n                    <svg className=\"animate-spin -ml-1 mr-2 h-4 w-4\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\">\n                      <circle className=\"opacity-25\" cx=\"12\" cy=\"12\" r=\"10\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth=\"4\" fill=\"none\" />\n                      <path className=\"opacity-75\" fill=\"currentColor\" d=\"M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z\" />\n                    </svg>\n                    Enviando template...\n                  </>\n                ) : (\n                  <>\n                    <Send className=\"w-4 h-4 mr-1\" />\n                    Enviar Template\n                  </>\n                )}\n              </button>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          // Window open but template picker collapsed\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={() => setShowTemplatePicker(true)}\n            className=\"w-full px-4 py-2 text-sm text-slate-400 hover:text-cyan-400 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg transition-colors\"\n          >\n            Usar template aprovado\n          </button>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Help text */}\n        <p className=\"text-[11px] text-slate-500 text-center\">\n          {isWindowOpen\n            ? \"Mensagens de texto livre s\u00e3o gr\u00e1tis dentro da janela de 24h ap\u00f3s o \u00faltimo inbound do cliente.\"\n            : \"Fora da janela de 24h, apenas templates aprovados pela Meta podem ser enviados. Templates MARKETING exigem consentimento do contato.\"}\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}"
}

channel?: string; search?: string };\n}\n\nexport function ConversationList({ conversations, selectedId, onSelect, filters }: ConversationListProps) {\n  const formatTime = (iso: string) => {\n    try {\n      const date = new Date(iso);\n      const now = new Date();\n      const diff = now.getTime() - date.getTime();\n      if (diff < 60000) return \"agora\";\n      if (diff < 3600000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 60000)}min`;\n      if (diff < 86400000) return `${Math.floor(diff / 3600000)}h`;\n      return date.toLocaleDateString(\"pt-BR\", { day: \"2-digit\", month: \"2-digit\" });\n    } catch {\n      return \"\u2014\";\n    }\n  };\n\n  const getStatusIcon = (conv: ConversationWithWindow) => {\n    if (conv.window_state === \"open\") return <CheckCircle className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400\" />;\n    if (conv.window_state === \"closing\") return <Clock className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400\" />;\n    return <AlertCircle className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500\" />;\n  };\n\n  const getStatusTooltip = (conv: ConversationWithWindow) => {\n    if (conv.window_state === \"open\") return \"Janela aberta (gr\u00e1tis)\";\n    if (conv.window_state === \"closing\") return \"Janela fechando em breve\";\n    return \"Janela fechada (apenas template)\";\n  };\n\n  if (conversations.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full text-slate-500 p-8\">\n        <MessageSquare className=\"w-12 h-12 mb-4 opacity-30\" />\n        <p className=\"text-slate-400\">Nenhuma conversa encontrada</p>\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1\">\n          {filters.search ? \"Tente ajustar sua busca\" : \"Conversas aparecer\u00e3o aqui quando chegarem mensagens\"}\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-950/50\">\n      {/* Filter bar */}\n      <div className=\"p-3 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center gap-2\">\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          placeholder=\"Buscar conversas...\"\n          defaultValue={filters.search}\n          onChange={(e) => { /* handled by parent via query */ }}\n          className=\"flex-1 bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-100 placeholder-slate-500 focus:border-cyan-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-400\"\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      {/* List */}\n      <div className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto\" role=\"listbox\" aria-label=\"Conversas\">\n        {conversations.map((conv) => (\n          <button\n            key={conv.id}\n            onClick={() => onSelect(conv.id)}\n            className={`w-full p-3 hover:bg-slate-800/50 transition-colors flex items-start gap-3 border-b border-slate-800/50 ${\n              selectedId === conv.id ? \"bg-cyan-500/5\" : \"\"\n            }`}\n            role=\"option\"\n            aria-selected={selectedId === conv.id}\n          >\n            {/* Window status indicator */}\n            <div className=\"flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5\" title={getStatusTooltip(conv)}>\n              {getStatusIcon(conv)}\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Conversation content */}\n            <div className=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n              <div className=\"flex items-start justify-between gap-2\">\n                <div className=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n                  <p className={`font-medium truncate ${selectedId === conv.id ? \"text-cyan-300\" : \"text-slate-100\"}`}>\n                    {conv.lead_name || conv.external_thread_key}\n                  </p>\n                  <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-500 truncate mt-0.5\">{conv.last_message_preview || \"\u2014\"}</p>\n                </div>\n                <div className=\"flex flex-col items-end gap-1 flex-shrink-0\">\n                  <span className=\"text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono\">{formatTime(conv.last_message_at)}</span>\n                  {conv.unread_count > 0 && (\n                    <span className=\"px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 rounded-full font-medium\">\n                      {conv.unread_count}\n                    </span>\n                  )}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n\n              {/* Status badge row */}\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-2\">\n                <span className=\"px-1.5 py-0 text-[10px] rounded ${\n                  conv.status === \"open\" ? \"bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300\" :\n                  conv.status === \"closed\" ? \"bg-slate-500/20 text-slate-300\" :\n                  \"bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300\"\n                }\">\n                  {conv.status}\n                </span>\n                <span className=\"px-1.5 py-0 text-[10px] bg-slate-700/50 text-slate-400 rounded\">\n                  WhatsApp\n                </span>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </button>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}"

Image, Mic, File, Send, Tag } from \"lucide-react\";\n\ninterface ThreadViewProps {\n  messages: MessageRow[];\n  conversation: {\n    id: string;\n    lead_name: string | null;\n    window_expires_at: string | null;\n    last_inbound_at: string | null;\n  } | null;\n}\n\nexport function ThreadView({ messages, conversation }: ThreadViewProps) {\n  const formatTime = (iso: string) => {\n    try {\n      return format(parseISO(iso), \"HH:mm\", { locale: ptBR });\n    } catch {\n      return \"\u2014\";\n    }\n  };\n\n  const formatDate = (iso: string) => {\n    try {\n      const date = parseISO(iso);\n      const today = new Date();\n      if (date.toDateString() === today.toDateString()) return \"Hoje\";\n      const yesterday = new Date(today.getTime() - 86400000);\n      if (date.toDateString() === yesterday.toDateString()) return \"Ontem\";\n      return format(date, \"dd/MM/yyyy\", { locale: ptBR });\n    } catch {\n      return \"\u2014\";\n    }\n  };\n\n  // Group messages by date\n  const groups = messages.reduce((acc: Record<string, MessageRow[]>, msg) => {\n    const date = formatDate(msg.created_at);\n    if (!acc[date]) acc[date] = [];\n    acc[date].push(msg);\n    return acc;\n  }, {});\n\n  const getContentIcon = (type: MessageRow[\"content_type\"]) => {\n    switch (type) {\n      case \"image\": return <Image className=\"w-4 h-4\" />;\n      case \"audio\": return <Mic className=\"w-4 h-4\" />;\n      case \"document\": return <File className=\"w-4 h-4\" />;\n      case \"template\": return <FileText className=\"w-4 h-4\" />;\n      default: return null;\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col h-full bg-slate-950/30 overflow-hidden\">\n      {/* Header */}\n      {conversation && (\n        <div className=\"px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-950/50\">\n          <div>\n            <h3 className=\"font-medium text-slate-100\">{conversation.lead_name || \"Conversa\"}</h3>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-500\">\n              {conversation.window_expires_at\n                ? new Date(conversation.window_expires_at) < new Date()\n                  ? \"Janela fechada \u2014 apenas templates\"\n                  : \"Janela aberta \u2014 mensagens gr\u00e1tis\"\n                : \"Sem janela ativa\"}\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Messages */}\n      <div className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-6\" role=\"log\" aria-live=\"polite\">\n        {Object.entries(groups).map(([date, msgs]) => (\n          <div key={date} className=\"space-y-4\">\n            {/* Date separator */}\n            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-slate-500 text-xs\">\n              <div className=\"flex-1 border-t border-slate-800\" />\n              <span className=\"px-2 bg-slate-950/30\">{date}</span>\n              <div className=\"flex-1 border-t border-slate-800\" />\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Messages */}\n            <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n              {msgs.map((msg) => (\n                <div\n                  key={msg.id}\n                  className={`flex gap-2 ${msg.direction === \"outbound\" ? \"flex-row-reverse\" : \"\"}`}\n                >\n                  {/* Avatar placeholder */}\n                  <div className={`flex-shrink-0 w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center ${msg.direction === \"inbound\" ? \"bg-slate-700\" : \"bg-cyan-500/20\"}`}>\n                    {msg.direction === \"inbound\" ? (\n                      <span className=\"text-xs font-medium text-slate-300\">\n                        {(msg.authored_by === \"system\" ? \"S\" : \"C\")[0]}\n                      </span>\n                    ) : (\n                      <Send className=\"w-4 h-4 text-cyan-400\" />\n                    )}\n                  </div>\n\n                  {/* Message bubble */}\n                  <div className={`max-w-[75%] ${msg.direction === \"outbound\" ? \"text-right\" : \"\"}`}>\n                    <div\n                      className={`inline-block px-4 py-2 rounded-2xl ${\n                        msg.direction === \"inbound\"\n                          ? \"bg-slate-800 text-slate-100 rounded-bl-sm\"\n                          : \"bg-cyan-500/10 text-slate-100 border border-cyan-500/20 rounded-br-sm\"\n                      }`}\n                    >\n                      {msg.content_type !== \"text\" && (\n                        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1\">\n                          {getContentIcon(msg.content_type)}\n                          <span className=\"capitalize\">{msg.content_type}</span>\n                        </div>\n                      )}\n\n                      {msg.content_type === \"template\" && msg.template_name && (\n                        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs mb-1\">\n                          <Tag className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-400\" />\n                          <span className=\"text-purple-300 font-medium\">{msg.template_name}</span>\n                          {msg.template_category && (\n                            <span className={`px-1 py-0 text-[9px] rounded ${\n                              msg.template_category === \"MARKETING\" ? \"bg-purple-500/20 text-purple-300\" :\n                              msg.template_category === \"UTILITY\" ? \"bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300\" :\n                              \"bg-green-500/20 text-green-300\"\n                            }`}>\n                              {msg.template_category}\n                            </span>\n                          )}\n                        </div>\n                      )}\n\n                      <p className=\"whitespace-pre-wrap break-words\">{msg.body || \"\u2014\"}</p>\n\n                      {/* Meta badges */}\n                      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5 mt-1.5 text-[10px] text-slate-500\">\n                        <time dateTime={msg.created_at}>{formatTime(msg.created_at)}</time>\n                        {msg.direction === \"outbound\" && (\n                          <>\n                            <span className=\"flex items-center gap-0.5\">\n                              <Send className=\"w-3 h-3\" />\n                              Enviado\n                            </span>\n                            {msg.provider_message_id && (\n                              <span className=\"font-mono\">{msg.provider_message_id.slice(0, 12)}\u2026</span>\n                            )}\n                            {msg.metadata.mco_charged && msg.metadata.mco_charged > 0 && (\n                              <span className=\"px-1 py-0 bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 rounded\">\n                                {msg.metadata.mco_charged} mco\n                              </span>\n                            )}\n                          </>\n                        )}\n                        {msg.authored_by === \"system\" && (\n                          <span className=\"px-1 py-0 bg-slate-700 text-slate-400 rounded\">Backfill</span>\n                        )}\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n\n        {/* Empty state */}\n        {messages.length === 0 && (\n          <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-64 text-slate-500\">\n            <FileText className=\"w-12 h-12 mb-4 opacity-30\" />\n            <p className=\"text-slate-400\">Nenhuma mensagem nesta conversa</p>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1\">A primeira mensagem aparecer\u00e1 aqui</p>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/useSendMessage.ts",
  "content": "import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { supabase } from \"@/integrations/supabase/client\";\nimport { edgeErrorMessage } from \"@/lib/edge\";\n\nexport interface SendMessageRequest {\n  conversationId: string;\n  to: string;\n  type: \"text\" | \"template\";\n  text?: string;\n  templateName?: string;\n  language?: string;\n  params?: string[];\n}\n\nexport interface SendMessageResponse {\n  ok: true;\n  message_id: string | null;\n  to: string;\n  mode: \"text\" | \"template\";\n  mco_charged: number;\n}\n\nexport function useSendMessage() {\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\n\n  return useMutation<SendMessageResponse, Error, SendMessageRequest>({\n    mutationFn: async (req) => {\n      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();\n      if (!session?.access_token) throw new Error(\"N\u00e3o autenticado\");\n\n      const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {\n        action: \"send\",\n        to: req.to,\n        conversation_id: req.conversationId,\n      };\n\n      if (req.type === \"text\") {\n        payload.type = \"text\";\n        payload.text = req.text;\n      } else {\n        payload.type = \"template\";\n        payload.template_name = req.templateName;\n        payload.language = req.language ?? \"pt_BR\";\n        payload.params = req.params ?? [];\n      }\n\n      const res = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/whatsapp-templates`, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: {\n          \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n          Authorization: `Bearer ${session.access_token}`,\n        },\n        body: JSON.stringify(payload),\n      });\n\n      const json = await res.json();\n      if (!res.ok) {\n        throw new Error(edgeErrorMessage(json) || json.error || \"Falha ao enviar\");\n      }\n      return json as SendMessageResponse;\n    },\n    onSuccess: (_data, variables) => {\n      // Invalidate messages for this conversation\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"messages\", variables.conversationId] });\n      // Invalidate conversations list (preview, unread count, last_message_at)\n      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [\"conversations\"] });\n    },\n  });\n}\n\n/** Template cost class helper for UI */\nexport function getTemplateCostClass(category: \"MARKETING\" | \"UTILITY\" | \"AUTHENTICATION\", windowOpen: boolean): \"free\" | \"paid\" {\n  if (windowOpen) return \"free\"; // UTILITY inside window = free (service message)\n  // Outside window: all templates are paid\n  return \"paid\";\n}\n\n/** Template cost in mcoCoins (SOP mcoin-cost-calibration 4\u00d7-floor) */\nexport function getTemplateCost(category: \"MARKETING\" | \"UTILITY\" | \"AUTHENTICATION\"): number {\n  // These values should match src/lib/billing.ts COIN_COSTS.whatsappTemplate\n  const costs: Record<string, number> = {\n    MARKETING: 40,\n    UTILITY: 20,\n    AUTHENTICATION: 10,\n  };\n  return costs[category] ?? 40;\n}"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/gcrUX/htdocs/constellation-orchestra/src/hooks/useTemplates.ts",
  "content": "import { useQuery, UseQueryResult } from \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { supabase } from \"@/integrations/supabase/client\";\n\nexport interface TemplateRow {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  status: string;\n  category: \"MARKETING\" | \"UTILITY\" | \"AUTHENTICATION\";\n  language: string;\n  components: Record<string, unknown>[];\n  quality_score?: \"HIGH\" | \"MEDIUM\" | \"LOW\" | \"UNKNOWN\";\n}\n\n/** Fetch approved templates for the current user's WABA */\nexport function useTemplates(): UseQueryResult<TemplateRow[], Error> {\n  return useQuery({\n    queryKey: [\"whatsapp-templates\"],\n    queryFn: async () => {\n      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();\n      if (!session?.access_token) throw new Error(\"N\u00e3o autenticado\");\n\n      const res = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/whatsapp-templates`, {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: {\n          \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n          Authorization: `Bearer ${session.access_token}`,\n        },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ action: \"list\" }),\n      });\n\n      const json = await res.json();\n      if (!res.ok) {\n        throw new Error(json.error || \"Falha ao carregar templates\");\n      }\n      // Filter only APPROVED templates for composer\n      return (json.templates ?? []).filter((t: TemplateRow) => t.status === \"APPROVED\");\n    },\n    staleTime: 60_000, // 1 min\n  });\n}\n\n/** Extract template body variables from components */\nexport function getTemplateVariables(template: TemplateRow): string[] {\n  const bodyComp = template.components?.find((c) => c.type === \"BODY\");\n  if (!bodyComp?.text) return [];\n  const matches = bodyComp.text.match(/\\{\\{(\\d+)\\}\\}/g);\n  if (!matches) return [];\n  return [...new Set(matches.map((m) => m.slice(2, -2)))].sort((a, b) => parseInt(a) - parseInt(b));\n}"
}
```
